SELECT CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [ScheduledSessions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[TrainerId] = @trainer_Id AND [s].[Status] IN (0, 1) AND [s].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit) AND [s].[ScheduledTime] < @AddMinutes AND DATEADD(minute, CAST(CAST([s].[DurationMinutes] AS float) AS int), [s].[ScheduledTime]) > @scheduledTime) THEN CAST(1 AS bit)
    ELSE CAST(0 AS bit)
END
